Entry 1
U: Would you mind helping me with a hotel reservation?

U: I'm planning to check out on March 9th.
A: Confirming 1 room at Ibis New Delhi Aerocity in Delhi for a March 2nd check in and a March 9th check out.
U: That's perfect, thanks.
A: The reservation has been booked.
U: What kind of rating do they have?
A: The hotel has a 4.1 average rating.
U: How much does it cost? And I also need a phone number.
A: The total price will be $1,344. The phone number of the hotel is +91 11 4302 0202.
U: Terrific. I also need a one way flight.
A: Where are you departing from?
U: Leaving from Vegas.
A: Is that on March 2nd?
U: Yes it is.
A: I've found 2 options. There's an American Airlines flight which leaves at 6:40 AM with 1 stop for $404.
U: What else is there?
A: I also have a Southwest Airlines flight at 9:55 AM, also with 1 stop for $378.
U: I think that one is good.
A: Would you like to buy tickets now?
U: Sure, and its for three people.
A: Confirming a flight on Southwest Airlines from Las Vegas to Delhi on March 2nd for 3 economy passengers.
U: Great, thank you. Where does the flight depart from and what is the arrival time of the onward flight?
A: It flies out of McCarran International Airport and the arrival is 4:07 PM. The reservation is confirmed.
U: Thank you for your help.
A: My pleasure, thank you.
